-/**

-
-namespace sitesplat\badge\conditions\type;
-
-/**
- * Badge Award Likedd class
- */
-class likedd extends \sitesplat\badge\conditions\type\base
-{
-	/**
-	 * Get condition type
-	 *
-	 * @return string Condition type
-	 * @access public
-	 */
-	public function get_condition_type()
-	{
-		return 'sitesplat.badge.type.likedd';
-	}
-
-	/**
-	 * Get condition boolean (either boolean or min max value)
-	 *
-	 * @return bool Condition boolean
-	 * @access public
-	 */
-	public function get_condition_boolean()
-	{
-		return false;
-	}
-		/**
-	 * Get condition field (this is the field to check)
-	 *
-	 * @return string Condition field name
-	 * @access public
-	 */
-	public function get_condition_field()
-	{
-		return 'likedd';
-	}
-
-	/**
-	 * Get condition type name
-	 *
-	 * @return string Condition type name
-	 * @access public
-	 */
-	public function get_condition_type_name()
-	{
-		return $this->user->lang('BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD');
-	}
-
-	/**
-	 * Get users to apply to this condition
-	 * Likedd is typically called via cron with no $options arguments.
-	 * By default, get all users, otherwise use user_id(s) supplied in $options arg.
-	 *
-	 * @param array $options Array of optional data
-	 * @return array Array of users ids as keys and their condition data as values
-	 * @access public
-	 */
-	 
-	/**	 
-	 * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
-	 * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
-	 * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
-	 * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
-	*/
-
-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())
-	{
-		$user_data = array();
-		$manager = $this->container->get('ext.manager');
-		if ($manager->is_enabled('sitesplat/likedd'))
-		{
-			// The user data this condition needs to check
-			$condition_data = array(
-				$this->get_condition_field(),
-			);
-	
-			// Merge default options, empty user array as the default
-			$options = array_merge(array(
-				'users'		=> array(),
-			), $options);
-	
-			// Prepare the user ids data for use in the query
-			$user_ids = $this->helper->prepare_users_for_query($options['users']);
-	
-			$this->likes_table = $this->container->getParameter('sitesplat.likedd.tables.likes');
-	
-			$option = (int) $options['option'];
-			$like_dislike =  $option < 2;
-			$give_recieve = ($option % 2) ? 0 : 1;
-	
-			// Get data for the users to be checked (exclude bots, guests and inactive users)
-			$sql = ($give_recieve) ? 'SELECT u.user_id, COUNT(CASE WHEN l.ld = ' . (($like_dislike) ? 0 : 1) . ' THEN l.user_id END) AS likedd
-				FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u
-				LEFT JOIN ' . POSTS_TABLE . ' p ON p.poster_id = u.user_id
-				LEFT JOIN ' . $this->likes_table . ' l ON l.post_id = p.post_id
-				WHERE 
-				' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
-					AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true) . '
-				group by u.user_id' :
-				'SELECT u.user_id, (SELECT COUNT(ld) FROM ' . $this->likes_table . ' WHERE ld = ' . (($like_dislike) ? 0 : 1) . ' AND user_id = u.user_id) AS likedd
-				FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u 
-				WHERE ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
-					AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true);
-			$result = $this->db->sql_query($sql);
-	
-			$user_data = array();
-			while ($row = $this->db->sql_fetchrow($result))
-			{
-				$user_data[$row['user_id']] = array(
-					$this->get_condition_field() => $row['likedd']
-				);
-			}
-			$this->db->sql_freeresult($result);
-		}
-		return $user_data;
-	}
-}

-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())

+ 					$this->get_condition_field() => $row['likedd']
+-				);
++				];
+ 			}
+ 			$this->db->sql_freeresult($result);
+ 		}

++++ /dev/null
+@@ -1,129 +0,0 @@
+-<?php
+-/**
+-*
+-* Badge Award extension for the phpBB Forum Software package.
+-*
+-* @copyright (c) 2015 SiteSplat All rights reserved
+-* @license Proprietary
+-*
+-*/
+-
+-namespace sitesplat\badge\conditions\type;
+-
+-/**
+- * Badge Award Likedd class
+- */
+-class likedd extends \sitesplat\badge\conditions\type\base
+-{
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition type
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition type
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_type()
+-	{
+-		return 'sitesplat.badge.type.likedd';
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition boolean (either boolean or min max value)
+-	 *
+-	 * @return bool Condition boolean
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_boolean()
+-	{
+-		return false;
+-	}
+-		/**
+-	 * Get condition field (this is the field to check)
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition field name
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_field()
+-	{
+-		return 'likedd';
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition type name
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition type name
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_type_name()
+-	{
+-		return $this->user->lang('BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD');
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get users to apply to this condition
+-	 * Likedd is typically called via cron with no $options arguments.
+-	 * By default, get all users, otherwise use user_id(s) supplied in $options arg.
+-	 *
+-	 * @param array $options Array of optional data
+-	 * @return array Array of users ids as keys and their condition data as values
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	 
+-	/**	 
+-	 * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
+-	 * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
+-	 * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
+-	 * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
+-	*/
+-
+-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())
+-	{
+-		$user_data = array();
+-		$manager = $this->container->get('ext.manager');
+-		if ($manager->is_enabled('sitesplat/likedd'))
+-		{
+-			// The user data this condition needs to check
+-			$condition_data = array(
+-				$this->get_condition_field(),
+-			);
+-	
+-			// Merge default options, empty user array as the default
+-			$options = array_merge(array(
+-				'users'		=> array(),
+-			), $options);
+-	
+-			// Prepare the user ids data for use in the query
+-			$user_ids = $this->helper->prepare_users_for_query($options['users']);
+-	
+-			$this->likes_table = $this->container->getParameter('sitesplat.likedd.tables.likes');
+-	
+-			$option = (int) $options['option'];
+-			$like_dislike =  $option < 2;
+-			$give_recieve = ($option % 2) ? 0 : 1;
+-	
+-			// Get data for the users to be checked (exclude bots, guests and inactive users)
+-			$sql = ($give_recieve) ? 'SELECT u.user_id, COUNT(CASE WHEN l.ld = ' . (($like_dislike) ? 0 : 1) . ' THEN l.user_id END) AS likedd
+-				FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u
+-				LEFT JOIN ' . POSTS_TABLE . ' p ON p.poster_id = u.user_id
+-				LEFT JOIN ' . $this->likes_table . ' l ON l.post_id = p.post_id
+-				WHERE 
+-				' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+-					AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true) . '
+-				group by u.user_id' :
+-				'SELECT u.user_id, (SELECT COUNT(ld) FROM ' . $this->likes_table . ' WHERE ld = ' . (($like_dislike) ? 0 : 1) . ' AND user_id = u.user_id) AS likedd
+-				FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u 
+-				WHERE ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+-					AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true);
+-			$result = $this->db->sql_query($sql);
+-	
+-			$user_data = array();
+-			while ($row = $this->db->sql_fetchrow($result))
+-			{
+-				$user_data[$row['user_id']] = array(
+-					$this->get_condition_field() => $row['likedd']
+-				);
+-			}
+-			$this->db->sql_freeresult($result);
+-		}
+-		return $user_data;
+-	}
+-}

+-<?php
+-/**
+-*
+-* Badge Award extension for the phpBB Forum Software package.

+-* @license Proprietary
+-*
+-*/
+-
+-namespace sitesplat\badge\conditions\type;
+-
+-/**
+- * Badge Award Likedd class
+- */
+-class likedd extends \sitesplat\badge\conditions\type\base
+-{
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition type
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition type
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_type()
+-	{
+-		return 'sitesplat.badge.type.likedd';
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition boolean (either boolean or min max value)
+-	 *
+-	 * @return bool Condition boolean
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_boolean()
+-	{
+-		return false;
+-	}
+-		/**
+-	 * Get condition field (this is the field to check)
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition field name
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_field()
+-	{
+-		return 'likedd';
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition type name
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition type name
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_type_name()
+-	{
+-		return $this->user->lang('BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD');
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get users to apply to this condition
+-	 * Likedd is typically called via cron with no $options arguments.
+-	 * By default, get all users, otherwise use user_id(s) supplied in $options arg.
+-	 *
+-	 * @param array $options Array of optional data
+-	 * @return array Array of users ids as keys and their condition data as values
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	 
+-	/**	 
+-	 * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
+-	 * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
+-	 * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
+-	 * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
+-	*/
+-
+-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())
+-	{
+-        $user_data = array();
+-		$manager = $this->container->get('ext.manager');
+-		if ($manager->is_enabled('sitesplat/likedd'))
+-		{
+-			// The user data this condition needs to check
+-			$condition_data = array(
+-				$this->get_condition_field(),
+-			);
+-            
+-
+-		// Merge default options, empty user array as the default
+-		$options = array_merge(array(
+-			'users'		=> array(),
+-		), $options);
+-
+-		// Prepare the user ids data for use in the query
+-		$user_ids = $this->helper->prepare_users_for_query($options['users']);
+-        
+-		$this->likes_table = $this->container->getParameter('sitesplat.likedd.tables.likes');
+- 
+-        /**	 
+-         * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
+-         * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
+-         * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
+-         * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
+-        */	
+-		$option = (int)$options['option'];
+-		if ($option < 2) {
+-            // dislike
+-            $like_dislike = 1;
+-        }
+-        else {
+-            // like
+-            $like_dislike = 0;
+-        }
+-        if (($option % 2) == 1) {
+-            $give_recieve = 0;
+-        }
+-        else {
+-            $give_recieve = 1;
+-        }
+-        
+-		// Get data for the users to be checked (exclude bots, guests and inactive users)
+-        // Case: (Dis)Likes RECEIVED
+-		$sql = ($give_recieve) ? 'SELECT u.user_id, COUNT(CASE WHEN l.ld = '.$like_dislike.' THEN l.user_id END) AS likedd
+-			FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u
+-			LEFT JOIN ' . POSTS_TABLE . ' p ON p.poster_id = u.user_id
+-			LEFT JOIN ' . $this->likes_table . ' l ON l.post_id = p.post_id
+-			WHERE 
+-			' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+-				AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true) . '
+-			group by u.user_id' :
+-            // Case: (Dis)Likes GIVEN
+-			'SELECT u.user_id, (SELECT COUNT(ld) FROM ' . $this->likes_table . ' WHERE ld = '.$like_dislike.' AND user_id = u.user_id) AS likedd
+-			FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u 
+-			WHERE ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+-				AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true);
+-		$result = $this->db->sql_query($sql);
+-
+-		$user_data = array();
+-		while ($row = $this->db->sql_fetchrow($result))
+-		{
+-			$user_data[$row['user_id']] = array(
+-				$this->get_condition_field() => $row['likedd']
+-			);
+-		}
+-		$this->db->sql_freeresult($result);
+-	}
+-	return $user_data;
+-	}
+-}

++-<?php
++-/**
++-*
++-* Badge Award extension for the phpBB Forum Software package.

++-namespace sitesplat\badge\conditions\type;
++-
++-/**
++- * Badge Award Likedd class
++- */
++-class likedd extends \sitesplat\badge\conditions\type\base
++-{
++-	/**
++-	 * Get condition type
++-	 *
++-	 * @return string Condition type
++-	 * @access public
++-	 */
++-	public function get_condition_type()
++-	{
++-		return 'sitesplat.badge.type.likedd';
++-	}
++-
++-	/**
++-	 * Get condition boolean (either boolean or min max value)
++-	 *
++-	 * @return bool Condition boolean
++-	 * @access public
++-	 */
++-	public function get_condition_boolean()
++-	{
++-		return false;
++-	}
++-		/**
++-	 * Get condition field (this is the field to check)
++-	 *
++-	 * @return string Condition field name
++-	 * @access public
++-	 */
++-	public function get_condition_field()
++-	{
++-		return 'likedd';
++-	}
++-
++-	/**
++-	 * Get condition type name
++-	 *
++-	 * @return string Condition type name
++-	 * @access public
++-	 */
++-	public function get_condition_type_name()
++-	{
++-		return $this->user->lang('BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD');
++-	}
++-
++-	/**
++-	 * Get users to apply to this condition
++-	 * Likedd is typically called via cron with no $options arguments.
++-	 * By default, get all users, otherwise use user_id(s) supplied in $options arg.
++-	 *
++-	 * @param array $options Array of optional data
++-	 * @return array Array of users ids as keys and their condition data as values
++-	 * @access public
++-	 */
++-	 
++-	/**	 
++-	 * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
++-	 * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
++-	 * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
++-	 * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
++-	*/
++-
++-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())
++-	{
++-		$user_data = array();
++-		$manager = $this->container->get('ext.manager');
++-		if ($manager->is_enabled('sitesplat/likedd'))
++-		{

++-	/**

++-		return false;

++-	 *

++-		return 'likedd';

++-
++-	/**

++-	{
++-		return $this->user->lang('BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD');
++-	}
++-
++-	/**
++-	 * Get users to apply to this condition
++-	 * Likedd is typically called via cron with no $options arguments.
++-	 * By default, get all users, otherwise use user_id(s) supplied in $options arg.
++-	 *
++-	 * @param array $options Array of optional data
++-	 * @return array Array of users ids as keys and their condition data as values
++-	 * @access public
++-	 */
++-	 
++-	/**	 
++-	 * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
++-	 * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
++-	 * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
++-	 * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
++-	*/
++-
++-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())
++-	{
++-        $user_data = array();
++-		$manager = $this->container->get('ext.manager');
++-		if ($manager->is_enabled('sitesplat/likedd'))
++-		{
++-			// The user data this condition needs to check
++-			$condition_data = array(
++-				$this->get_condition_field(),
++-			);
++-            
++-
++-		// Merge default options, empty user array as the default
++-		$options = array_merge(array(
++-			'users'		=> array(),
++-		), $options);
++-
++-		// Prepare the user ids data for use in the query
++-		$user_ids = $this->helper->prepare_users_for_query($options['users']);
++-        
++-		$this->likes_table = $this->container->getParameter('sitesplat.likedd.tables.likes');
++- 
++-        /**	 
++-         * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
++-         * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
++-         * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
++-         * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
++-        */	
++-		$option = (int)$options['option'];
++-		if ($option < 2) {
++-            // dislike
++-            $like_dislike = 1;
++-        }
++-        else {
++-            // like
++-            $like_dislike = 0;
++-        }
++-        if (($option % 2) == 1) {
++-            $give_recieve = 0;
++-        }
++-        else {
++-            $give_recieve = 1;
++-        }
++-        
++-		// Get data for the users to be checked (exclude bots, guests and inactive users)
++-        // Case: (Dis)Likes RECEIVED
++-		$sql = ($give_recieve) ? 'SELECT u.user_id, COUNT(CASE WHEN l.ld = '.$like_dislike.' THEN l.user_id END) AS likedd
++-			FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u
++-			LEFT JOIN ' . POSTS_TABLE . ' p ON p.poster_id = u.user_id
++-			LEFT JOIN ' . $this->likes_table . ' l ON l.post_id = p.post_id
++-			WHERE 
++-			' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
++-				AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true) . '
++-			group by u.user_id' :
++-            // Case: (Dis)Likes GIVEN
++-			'SELECT u.user_id, (SELECT COUNT(ld) FROM ' . $this->likes_table . ' WHERE ld = '.$like_dislike.' AND user_id = u.user_id) AS likedd
++-			FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u 
++-			WHERE ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
++-				AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true);
++-		$result = $this->db->sql_query($sql);
++-
++-		$user_data = array();
++-		while ($row = $this->db->sql_fetchrow($result))
++-		{
++-			$user_data[$row['user_id']] = array(
++-				$this->get_condition_field() => $row['likedd']
++-			);
++-		}
++-		$this->db->sql_freeresult($result);
++-	}
++-	return $user_data;
++-	}
++-}

++* These are errors which can be triggered by sending invalid data to the

++* the core badge extension code OR unless they are writing an extension

++
++	'BADGE_TYPE_NOT_EXIST'		=> 'O `%1$s` não existe',
++));

++/*
++* These are errors which can be triggered by sending invalid data to the
++* badge extension API.
++*
++* These errors will never show to a user unless they are either modifying
++* the core badge extension code OR unless they are writing an extension
++* which makes calls to this extension.

++* Translators: Feel free to not translate these language strings
++*/

++	'EXCEPTION_FIELD_MISSING'		=> 'Campo obrigatório em falta',

++	'EXCEPTION_ILLEGAL_CHARACTERS'	=> 'o input contém caracteres proibidos.',
++
++	'BADGE_TYPE_NOT_EXIST'		=> 'O `%1$s` não existe',
++));

++	/**
++	 * Get the user's avatar
++	 */
++	public function get_avatar()
++	{
++		// Define a mapping of badge types to corresponding image URLs
++		$badgeTypeToImageMap = array(
++			'BADGE_TYPE_RADAR' => 'radar.png',
++			// Add more mappings as needed
++		);
++
++		// Check if the badge type is in the mapping
++		if (isset($badgeTypeToImageMap[$this->get_data('rule_name')])) {
++			// Get the corresponding image URL
++			$imageURL = $badgeTypeToImageMap[$this->get_data('rule_name')];
++		} else {
++			// Use a default image URL if not found in the mapping
++			$imageURL = 'default.png';
++		}
++		
++		$url = generate_board_url();
++		
++		
++		// Build the URL to the badge image
++		$badgeImagePath = $url . '/ext/sitesplat/badge/badges/' . $imageURL;
++
++		// Return an HTML image tag with the badge image
++		return '<img src="' . $badgeImagePath . '" alt="Badge ' . $this->user->lang[$this->get_data('rule_name')] . '" />';
++	}
++

++++ /dev/null
+@@ -1,129 +0,0 @@
+-<?php
+-/**
+-*
+-* Badge Award extension for the phpBB Forum Software package.
+-*
+-* @copyright (c) 2015 SiteSplat All rights reserved
+-* @license Proprietary
+-*
+-*/
+-
+-namespace sitesplat\badge\conditions\type;
+-
+-/**
+- * Badge Award Likedd class
+- */
+-class likedd extends \sitesplat\badge\conditions\type\base
+-{
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition type
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition type
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_type()
+-	{
+-		return 'sitesplat.badge.type.likedd';
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition boolean (either boolean or min max value)
+-	 *
+-	 * @return bool Condition boolean
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_boolean()
+-	{
+-		return false;
+-	}
+-		/**
+-	 * Get condition field (this is the field to check)
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition field name
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_field()
+-	{
+-		return 'likedd';
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition type name
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition type name
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_type_name()
+-	{
+-		return $this->user->lang('BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD');
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get users to apply to this condition
+-	 * Likedd is typically called via cron with no $options arguments.
+-	 * By default, get all users, otherwise use user_id(s) supplied in $options arg.
+-	 *
+-	 * @param array $options Array of optional data
+-	 * @return array Array of users ids as keys and their condition data as values
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	 
+-	/**	 
+-	 * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
+-	 * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
+-	 * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
+-	 * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
+-	*/
+-
+-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())
+-	{
+-		$user_data = array();
+-		$manager = $this->container->get('ext.manager');
+-		if ($manager->is_enabled('sitesplat/likedd'))
+-		{
+-			// The user data this condition needs to check
+-			$condition_data = array(
+-				$this->get_condition_field(),
+-			);
+-	
+-			// Merge default options, empty user array as the default
+-			$options = array_merge(array(
+-				'users'		=> array(),
+-			), $options);
+-	
+-			// Prepare the user ids data for use in the query
+-			$user_ids = $this->helper->prepare_users_for_query($options['users']);
+-	
+-			$this->likes_table = $this->container->getParameter('sitesplat.likedd.tables.likes');
+-	
+-			$option = (int) $options['option'];
+-			$like_dislike =  $option < 2;
+-			$give_recieve = ($option % 2) ? 0 : 1;
+-	
+-			// Get data for the users to be checked (exclude bots, guests and inactive users)
+-			$sql = ($give_recieve) ? 'SELECT u.user_id, COUNT(CASE WHEN l.ld = ' . (($like_dislike) ? 0 : 1) . ' THEN l.user_id END) AS likedd
+-				FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u
+-				LEFT JOIN ' . POSTS_TABLE . ' p ON p.poster_id = u.user_id
+-				LEFT JOIN ' . $this->likes_table . ' l ON l.post_id = p.post_id
+-				WHERE 
+-				' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+-					AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true) . '
+-				group by u.user_id' :
+-				'SELECT u.user_id, (SELECT COUNT(ld) FROM ' . $this->likes_table . ' WHERE ld = ' . (($like_dislike) ? 0 : 1) . ' AND user_id = u.user_id) AS likedd
+-				FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u 
+-				WHERE ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+-					AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true);
+-			$result = $this->db->sql_query($sql);
+-	
+-			$user_data = array();
+-			while ($row = $this->db->sql_fetchrow($result))
+-			{
+-				$user_data[$row['user_id']] = array(
+-					$this->get_condition_field() => $row['likedd']
+-				);
+-			}
+-			$this->db->sql_freeresult($result);
+-		}
+-		return $user_data;
+-	}
+-}
+diff --git "a/c:\\Users\\Leinad4Mind\\Documents\\GitHub\\gold-phpbb-ext\\sitesplat\\badge/conditions/type/likedd_fix_for_iveco2.php" "b/c:\\Users\\Leinad4Mind\\Documents\\GitHub\\gold-phpbb-ext\\sitesplat\\badge/conditions/type/likedd_fix_for_iveco2.php"

+--- "a/c:\\Users\\Leinad4Mind\\Documents\\GitHub\\gold-phpbb-ext\\sitesplat\\badge/conditions/type/likedd_fix_for_iveco2.php"
++++ /dev/null
+@@ -1,150 +0,0 @@
+-<?php
+-/**
+-*
+-* Badge Award extension for the phpBB Forum Software package.
+-*
+-* @copyright (c) 2015 SiteSplat All rights reserved
+-* @license Proprietary
+-*
+-*/
+-
+-namespace sitesplat\badge\conditions\type;
+-
+-/**
+- * Badge Award Likedd class
+- */
+-class likedd extends \sitesplat\badge\conditions\type\base
+-{
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition type
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition type
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_type()
+-	{
+-		return 'sitesplat.badge.type.likedd';
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition boolean (either boolean or min max value)
+-	 *
+-	 * @return bool Condition boolean
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_boolean()
+-	{
+-		return false;
+-	}
+-		/**
+-	 * Get condition field (this is the field to check)
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition field name
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_field()
+-	{
+-		return 'likedd';
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get condition type name
+-	 *
+-	 * @return string Condition type name
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	public function get_condition_type_name()
+-	{
+-		return $this->user->lang('BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD');
+-	}
+-
+-	/**
+-	 * Get users to apply to this condition
+-	 * Likedd is typically called via cron with no $options arguments.
+-	 * By default, get all users, otherwise use user_id(s) supplied in $options arg.
+-	 *
+-	 * @param array $options Array of optional data
+-	 * @return array Array of users ids as keys and their condition data as values
+-	 * @access public
+-	 */
+-	 
+-	/**	 
+-	 * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
+-	 * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
+-	 * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
+-	 * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
+-	*/
+-
+-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())
+-	{
+-        $user_data = array();
+-		$manager = $this->container->get('ext.manager');
+-		if ($manager->is_enabled('sitesplat/likedd'))
+-		{
+-			// The user data this condition needs to check
+-			$condition_data = array(
+-				$this->get_condition_field(),
+-			);
+-            
+-
+-		// Merge default options, empty user array as the default
+-		$options = array_merge(array(
+-			'users'		=> array(),
+-		), $options);
+-
+-		// Prepare the user ids data for use in the query
+-		$user_ids = $this->helper->prepare_users_for_query($options['users']);
+-        
+-		$this->likes_table = $this->container->getParameter('sitesplat.likedd.tables.likes');
+- 
+-        /**	 
+-         * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
+-         * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
+-         * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
+-         * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
+-        */	
+-		$option = (int)$options['option'];
+-		if ($option < 2) {
+-            // dislike
+-            $like_dislike = 1;
+-        }
+-        else {
+-            // like
+-            $like_dislike = 0;
+-        }
+-        if (($option % 2) == 1) {
+-            $give_recieve = 0;
+-        }
+-        else {
+-            $give_recieve = 1;
+-        }
+-        
+-		// Get data for the users to be checked (exclude bots, guests and inactive users)
+-        // Case: (Dis)Likes RECEIVED
+-		$sql = ($give_recieve) ? 'SELECT u.user_id, COUNT(CASE WHEN l.ld = '.$like_dislike.' THEN l.user_id END) AS likedd
+-			FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u
+-			LEFT JOIN ' . POSTS_TABLE . ' p ON p.poster_id = u.user_id
+-			LEFT JOIN ' . $this->likes_table . ' l ON l.post_id = p.post_id
+-			WHERE 
+-			' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+-				AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true) . '
+-			group by u.user_id' :
+-            // Case: (Dis)Likes GIVEN
+-			'SELECT u.user_id, (SELECT COUNT(ld) FROM ' . $this->likes_table . ' WHERE ld = '.$like_dislike.' AND user_id = u.user_id) AS likedd
+-			FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u 
+-			WHERE ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+-				AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true);
+-		$result = $this->db->sql_query($sql);
+-
+-		$user_data = array();
+-		while ($row = $this->db->sql_fetchrow($result))
+-		{
+-			$user_data[$row['user_id']] = array(
+-				$this->get_condition_field() => $row['likedd']
+-			);
+-		}
+-		$this->db->sql_freeresult($result);
+-	}
+-	return $user_data;
+-	}
+-}

+++--- "a/c:\\Users\\Leinad4Mind\\Documents\\GitHub\\gold-phpbb-ext\\sitesplat\\badge/conditions/type/likedd_fix_for_iveco.php"
++++++ /dev/null
+++@@ -1,129 +0,0 @@
+++-<?php
+++-/**
+++-*
+++-* Badge Award extension for the phpBB Forum Software package.
+++-*
+++-* @copyright (c) 2015 SiteSplat All rights reserved
+++-* @license Proprietary
+++-*
+++-*/
+++-
+++-namespace sitesplat\badge\conditions\type;
+++-
+++-/**
+++- * Badge Award Likedd class
+++- */
+++-class likedd extends \sitesplat\badge\conditions\type\base
+++-{
+++-	/**
+++-	 * Get condition type
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @return string Condition type
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	public function get_condition_type()
+++-	{
+++-		return 'sitesplat.badge.type.likedd';
+++-	}
+++-
+++-	/**
+++-	 * Get condition boolean (either boolean or min max value)
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @return bool Condition boolean
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	public function get_condition_boolean()
+++-	{
+++-		return false;
+++-	}
+++-		/**
+++-	 * Get condition field (this is the field to check)
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @return string Condition field name
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	public function get_condition_field()
+++-	{
+++-		return 'likedd';
+++-	}
+++-
+++-	/**
+++-	 * Get condition type name
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @return string Condition type name
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	public function get_condition_type_name()
+++-	{
+++-		return $this->user->lang('BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD');
+++-	}
+++-
+++-	/**
+++-	 * Get users to apply to this condition
+++-	 * Likedd is typically called via cron with no $options arguments.
+++-	 * By default, get all users, otherwise use user_id(s) supplied in $options arg.
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @param array $options Array of optional data
+++-	 * @return array Array of users ids as keys and their condition data as values
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	 
+++-	/**	 
+++-	 * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
+++-	 * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
+++-	 * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
+++-	 * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
+++-	*/
+++-
+++-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())
+++-	{
+++-		$user_data = array();
+++-		$manager = $this->container->get('ext.manager');
+++-		if ($manager->is_enabled('sitesplat/likedd'))
+++-		{
+++-			// The user data this condition needs to check
+++-			$condition_data = array(
+++-				$this->get_condition_field(),
+++-			);
+++-	
+++-			// Merge default options, empty user array as the default
+++-			$options = array_merge(array(
+++-				'users'		=> array(),
+++-			), $options);
+++-	
+++-			// Prepare the user ids data for use in the query
+++-			$user_ids = $this->helper->prepare_users_for_query($options['users']);
+++-	
+++-			$this->likes_table = $this->container->getParameter('sitesplat.likedd.tables.likes');
+++-	
+++-			$option = (int) $options['option'];
+++-			$like_dislike =  $option < 2;
+++-			$give_recieve = ($option % 2) ? 0 : 1;
+++-	
+++-			// Get data for the users to be checked (exclude bots, guests and inactive users)
+++-			$sql = ($give_recieve) ? 'SELECT u.user_id, COUNT(CASE WHEN l.ld = ' . (($like_dislike) ? 0 : 1) . ' THEN l.user_id END) AS likedd
+++-				FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u
+++-				LEFT JOIN ' . POSTS_TABLE . ' p ON p.poster_id = u.user_id
+++-				LEFT JOIN ' . $this->likes_table . ' l ON l.post_id = p.post_id
+++-				WHERE 
+++-				' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+++-					AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true) . '
+++-				group by u.user_id' :
+++-				'SELECT u.user_id, (SELECT COUNT(ld) FROM ' . $this->likes_table . ' WHERE ld = ' . (($like_dislike) ? 0 : 1) . ' AND user_id = u.user_id) AS likedd
+++-				FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u 
+++-				WHERE ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+++-					AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true);
+++-			$result = $this->db->sql_query($sql);
+++-	
+++-			$user_data = array();
+++-			while ($row = $this->db->sql_fetchrow($result))
+++-			{
+++-				$user_data[$row['user_id']] = array(
+++-					$this->get_condition_field() => $row['likedd']
+++-				);
+++-			}
+++-			$this->db->sql_freeresult($result);
+++-		}
+++-		return $user_data;
+++-	}
+++-}

++++++ /dev/null
+++@@ -1,150 +0,0 @@
+++-<?php
+++-/**
+++-*
+++-* Badge Award extension for the phpBB Forum Software package.
+++-*
+++-* @copyright (c) 2015 SiteSplat All rights reserved
+++-* @license Proprietary
+++-*
+++-*/
+++-
+++-namespace sitesplat\badge\conditions\type;
+++-
+++-/**
+++- * Badge Award Likedd class
+++- */
+++-class likedd extends \sitesplat\badge\conditions\type\base
+++-{
+++-	/**
+++-	 * Get condition type
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @return string Condition type
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	public function get_condition_type()
+++-	{
+++-		return 'sitesplat.badge.type.likedd';
+++-	}
+++-
+++-	/**
+++-	 * Get condition boolean (either boolean or min max value)
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @return bool Condition boolean
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	public function get_condition_boolean()
+++-	{
+++-		return false;
+++-	}
+++-		/**
+++-	 * Get condition field (this is the field to check)
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @return string Condition field name
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	public function get_condition_field()
+++-	{
+++-		return 'likedd';
+++-	}
+++-
+++-	/**
+++-	 * Get condition type name
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @return string Condition type name
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	public function get_condition_type_name()
+++-	{
+++-		return $this->user->lang('BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD');
+++-	}
+++-
+++-	/**
+++-	 * Get users to apply to this condition
+++-	 * Likedd is typically called via cron with no $options arguments.
+++-	 * By default, get all users, otherwise use user_id(s) supplied in $options arg.
+++-	 *
+++-	 * @param array $options Array of optional data
+++-	 * @return array Array of users ids as keys and their condition data as values
+++-	 * @access public
+++-	 */
+++-	 
+++-	/**	 
+++-	 * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
+++-	 * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
+++-	 * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
+++-	 * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
+++-	*/
+++-
+++-	public function get_users_for_condition($options = array())
+++-	{
+++-        $user_data = array();
+++-		$manager = $this->container->get('ext.manager');
+++-		if ($manager->is_enabled('sitesplat/likedd'))
+++-		{
+++-			// The user data this condition needs to check
+++-			$condition_data = array(
+++-				$this->get_condition_field(),
+++-			);
+++-            
+++-
+++-		// Merge default options, empty user array as the default
+++-		$options = array_merge(array(
+++-			'users'		=> array(),
+++-		), $options);
+++-
+++-		// Prepare the user ids data for use in the query
+++-		$user_ids = $this->helper->prepare_users_for_query($options['users']);
+++-        
+++-		$this->likes_table = $this->container->getParameter('sitesplat.likedd.tables.likes');
+++- 
+++-        /**	 
+++-         * DISLIKE RECEIVED - Option = 0
+++-         * DISLIKE GIVEN - Option = 1
+++-         * LIKE RECEIVED - Option = 2
+++-         * LIKE GIVEN - Option = 3
+++-        */	
+++-		$option = (int)$options['option'];
+++-		if ($option < 2) {
+++-            // dislike
+++-            $like_dislike = 1;
+++-        }
+++-        else {
+++-            // like
+++-            $like_dislike = 0;
+++-        }
+++-        if (($option % 2) == 1) {
+++-            $give_recieve = 0;
+++-        }
+++-        else {
+++-            $give_recieve = 1;
+++-        }
+++-        
+++-		// Get data for the users to be checked (exclude bots, guests and inactive users)
+++-        // Case: (Dis)Likes RECEIVED
+++-		$sql = ($give_recieve) ? 'SELECT u.user_id, COUNT(CASE WHEN l.ld = '.$like_dislike.' THEN l.user_id END) AS likedd
+++-			FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u
+++-			LEFT JOIN ' . POSTS_TABLE . ' p ON p.poster_id = u.user_id
+++-			LEFT JOIN ' . $this->likes_table . ' l ON l.post_id = p.post_id
+++-			WHERE 
+++-			' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+++-				AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true) . '
+++-			group by u.user_id' :
+++-            // Case: (Dis)Likes GIVEN
+++-			'SELECT u.user_id, (SELECT COUNT(ld) FROM ' . $this->likes_table . ' WHERE ld = '.$like_dislike.' AND user_id = u.user_id) AS likedd
+++-			FROM ' . USERS_TABLE . ' u 
+++-			WHERE ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_type', array(USER_INACTIVE, USER_IGNORE), true) . '
+++-				AND ' . $this->db->sql_in_set('u.user_id', $user_ids, !sizeof($user_ids), true);
+++-		$result = $this->db->sql_query($sql);
+++-
+++-		$user_data = array();
+++-		while ($row = $this->db->sql_fetchrow($result))
+++-		{
+++-			$user_data[$row['user_id']] = array(
+++-				$this->get_condition_field() => $row['likedd']
+++-			);
+++-		}
+++-		$this->db->sql_freeresult($result);
+++-	}
+++-	return $user_data;
+++-	}
+++-}

+++// DEVELOPERS PLEASE NOTE
+++//
+++// All language files should use UTF-8 as their encoding and the files must not contain a BOM.
+++//
+++// Placeholders can now contain order information, e.g. instead of
+++// 'Page %s of %s' you can (and should) write 'Page %1$s of %2$s', this allows
+++// translators to re-order the output of data while ensuring it remains correct
+++//
+++// You do not need this where single placeholders are used, e.g. 'Message %d' is fine
+++// equally where a string contains only two placeholders which are used to wrap text
+++// in a url you again do not need to specify an order e.g., 'Click %sHERE%s' is fine

+++	/**
+++	 * Get the user's avatar
+++	 */
+++	public function get_avatar()
+++	{
+++		// Define a mapping of badge types to corresponding image URLs
+++		$badgeTypeToImageMap = array(
+++			'BADGE_TYPE_RADAR' => 'radar.png',
+++			// Add more mappings as needed
+++		);
+++
+++		// Check if the badge type is in the mapping
+++		if (isset($badgeTypeToImageMap[$this->get_data('rule_name')])) {
+++			// Get the corresponding image URL
+++			$imageURL = $badgeTypeToImageMap[$this->get_data('rule_name')];
+++		} else {
+++			// Use a default image URL if not found in the mapping
+++			$imageURL = 'default.png';
+++		}
+++		
+++		$url = generate_board_url();
+++		
+++		
+++		// Build the URL to the badge image
+++		$badgeImagePath = $url . '/ext/sitesplat/badge/badges/' . $imageURL;
+++
+++		// Return an HTML image tag with the badge image
+++		return '<img src="' . $badgeImagePath . '" alt="Badge ' . $this->user->lang[$this->get_data('rule_name')] . '" />';
+++	}
+++

+$lang = array_merge($lang, array(

+	'ACP_BADGE_MANAGE_EXPLAIN'			=> 'Bem-vindo ao Sistema de Medalhas "BBAwards". No formulário abaixo poderás adicionar, editar, ver e remover Regras de Medalhas. <br />Poderás também enviar novas medalhas directamente para o servidor para que possam ser usadas na configuração das regras.',
+    'ACP_BADGE_MANAGE_LEGEND_TITLE'	    => 'Notas de Utilização',
+	'ACP_BADGE_MANAGE_LEGEND_EXPLAIN'	=> 'As regras de medalhas do utilizador são automaticamente sincronizadas a cada 24h pelo sistema especial de Cron (Podes monitorizar os Crons via extensão <a href="https://github.com/ForumHulp/cronstatus">Cron Status</a>) Serve para ficarmos de olho no load do servidor. <br />Algumas regras são ganhas instantaneamente de acordo com critérios, tal como as regras de "mensagens", "avisos", "dias de registo", "gostos", "customizado" e "donativos".<br />Podes editar, remover e sincronizar manualmente as regras rules nas opções da coluna "Acções". A opção de sincronização <i class="fa fa-refresh fa-lg"></i> é para executar manualmente a regra X instantaneamente. A regra "Customizada" não precisa de ser sincronizada, poils ela já se encontra sincronizada ao atribuírmos o utilizador à regra.',

+	'ACP_BADGE_NOTICE_CLOSE'			=> 'Close notification',

+/**

+// DEVELOPERS PLEASE NOTE

+// All language files should use UTF-8 as their encoding and the files must not contain a BOM.
+//
+// Placeholders can now contain order information, e.g. instead of
+// 'Page %s of %s' you can (and should) write 'Page %1$s of %2$s', this allows
+// translators to re-order the output of data while ensuring it remains correct
+//
+// You do not need this where single placeholders are used, e.g. 'Message %d' is fine
+// equally where a string contains only two placeholders which are used to wrap text
+// in a url you again do not need to specify an order e.g., 'Click %sHERE%s' is fine

+// ’ » “ ” …

+
+$lang = array_merge($lang, array(
+	'BADGE_MANAGE'				=> 'Medalhas',
+	'BADGE_MANAGE_EXPLAIN'		=> 'Vista geral de todas as medalhas ganhas',
+	'BADGE_RECENT'				=> 'Prémios Recentes',

+	'BADGE_INTRO'				=> 'Seja por teres ganho um passatempo, jogo, ajudado a comunidade, ou por apenas teres participado, estamos felizes por celebrar a tua contribuição para com a nossa comunidade.',
+	'BADGE_AWARDS_TOPIC'		=> 'Prémio',
+	'BADGE_WALL_TITLE'			=> 'O meu mural de medalhas',
+	
+	'UCP_BADGE_TITLE'			=> 'Medalhas',
+	'UCP_BADGE_EXPLAIN'			=> 'Vista geral de todas as minhas medalhas',
+
+	// Conditions
+	'BADGE_TYPE_AVATAR'			=> 'Avatar',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_BADGES'			=> 'Medalhas Ganhas',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_BIRTHDAYS'		=> 'Idade do Utilizdor',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_CUSTOM'			=> 'Especial',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_DONATIONS'		=> 'Donativos',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_LIKEDD'			=> 'Gostos',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_MEMBERSHIP'		=> 'Dias de Registo',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_POSTS'			=> 'Mensagens',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_PROFILEWALL'	=> 'Mural de Perfil',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_SIGNATURE'		=> 'Assinatura',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_WARNINGS'		=> 'Avisos',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_ZEBRA_FOES'		=> 'Inimigos',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_ZEBRA_FRIENDS'	=> 'Amigos',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_RADAR'			=> 'Radar',
+
+	'BADGE_USED'				=> 'usado',
+	'BADGE_BETWEEN'			=> 'entre',
+	'BADGE_AND'					=> 'e',
+	'BADGE_MORE'				=> 'igual ou maior que',
+	'MORE_BADGES'				=> '<a class="badge-more" href="%1$s"><span class="hidden-xs">&#43;%2$s mais</span><i class="visible-xs-inline-block fa fa-plus-circle"></i></a>',
+
+	// Notifications
+	'BADGE_NOTIFICATION_EVENT_ADDED'	=> '<strong>Ganhaste uma nova medalha!</strong><br />Uma nova medalha %1$s foi adicionada ao teu mural.',

diff --git "[FORUM_EXT]/sitesplat\\badge/language/pt/exceptions.php" "[FORUM_EXT]/sitesplat\\badge/language/pt/exceptions.php"
new file mode 100644
--- /dev/null
+++ "[FORUM_EXT]/sitesplat\\badge/language/pt/exceptions.php"
@@ -0,0 +1,60 @@
+<?php
+/**
+*
+* Badge Award extension for the phpBB Forum Software package.
+*
+* @copyright (c) 2015 SiteSplat All rights reserved
+* @license Proprietary
+*
+*/
+
+/**
+* DO NOT CHANGE
+*/
+if (!defined('IN_PHPBB'))
+{
+	exit;
+}
+
+if (empty($lang) || !is_array($lang))
+{
+	$lang = array();
+}
+
+// DEVELOPERS PLEASE NOTE
+//
+// All language files should use UTF-8 as their encoding and the files must not contain a BOM.
+//
+// Placeholders can now contain order information, e.g. instead of
+// 'Page %s of %s' you can (and should) write 'Page %1$s of %2$s', this allows
+// translators to re-order the output of data while ensuring it remains correct
+//
+// You do not need this where single placeholders are used, e.g. 'Message %d' is fine
+// equally where a string contains only two placeholders which are used to wrap text
+// in a url you again do not need to specify an order e.g., 'Click %sHERE%s' is fine
+//
+// Some characters you may want to copy&paste:
+// ’ » “ ” …
+//
+
+/*
+* These are errors which can be triggered by sending invalid data to the
+* badge extension API.
+*
+* These errors will never show to a user unless they are either modifying
+* the core badge extension code OR unless they are writing an extension
+* which makes calls to this extension.
+*
+* Translators: Feel free to not translate these language strings
+*/
+$lang = array_merge($lang, array(
+	'EXCEPTION_FIELD_MISSING'		=> 'Campo obrigatório em falta',
+	'EXCEPTION_INVALID_ARGUMENT'	=> 'Foi especificado um argumento inválido para `%1$s`. Motivo: %2$s',
+	'EXCEPTION_OUT_OF_BOUNDS'		=> 'O campo `%1$s` recebeu dados para além do esperado',
+	'EXCEPTION_TOO_LONG'			=> 'O input foi maior que o tamanho máximo.',
+	'EXCEPTION_NOT_UNIQUE'			=> 'O input não foi único.',
+	'EXCEPTION_UNEXPECTED_VALUE'	=> 'O campo `%1$s` recebeu dados inesperados. Motivo: %2$s',
+	'EXCEPTION_ILLEGAL_CHARACTERS'	=> 'o input contém caracteres proibidos.',
+
+	'BADGE_TYPE_NOT_EXIST'		=> 'O `%1$s` não existe',
+));

+$lang = array_merge($lang, array(

+	// Notifications

diff --git "[FORUM_EXT]/sitesplat\\badge/language/pt_preao/info_acp_badge.php" "[FORUM_EXT]/sitesplat\\badge/language/pt_preao/info_acp_badge.php"

+<?php
+/**
+*
+* Badge Award extension for the phpBB Forum Software package.

+* @copyright (c) 2015 SiteSplat All rights reserved
+* @license Proprietary
+*
+*/
+
+/**
+* DO NOT CHANGE
+*/
+if (!defined('IN_PHPBB'))
+{
+	exit;
+}
+
+if (empty($lang) || !is_array($lang))
+{
+	$lang = array();
+}
+
+// DEVELOPERS PLEASE NOTE
+//
+// All language files should use UTF-8 as their encoding and the files must not contain a BOM.
+//
+// Placeholders can now contain order information, e.g. instead of
+// 'Page %s of %s' you can (and should) write 'Page %1$s of %2$s', this allows
+// translators to re-order the output of data while ensuring it remains correct
+//
+// You do not need this where single placeholders are used, e.g. 'Message %d' is fine
+// equally where a string contains only two placeholders which are used to wrap text
+// in a url you again do not need to specify an order e.g., 'Click %sHERE%s' is fine
+//
+// Some characters you may want to copy&paste:
+// ’ » “ ” …
+//
+
+$lang = array_merge($lang, array(
+	'ACP_BADGE_MANAGE'			=> 'Gerir Sistema de Medalhas',
+	'BADGE_TYPE_NOT_EXIST'		=> 'O tipo de Medalha `%1$s` não existe.',
+	'BADGE_NOTICE'				=> '<div class="phpinfo"><p>As configurações desta extensão encontram-se em <strong>%1$s &#187; %2$s &#187; %3$s</strong>.<br /><strong>Nota:</strong> Donativos PayPal, Sistema de Gostos (LikeBB) e muitos outros suportados. Regras da Extensão Premium <strong>apenas</strong> estarão disponíveis se essas extensões estiveram ativas!</p></div>',
+	'SS_HELPER_NOTY'			=> 'O Sistema Core SiteSplat (BBcore) não se encontra instalado!<br /> Transfere o <a href="https://sitesplat.com" target="_blank">BBCore</a> e copia a pasta BBcore para dentro da pasta de extensões sitesplat.',
+	'ERROR_BADGE_DISABLE'		=> 'Não podes desactivar o Sistema de Medalhas por teres outras extensões com dependências:<br /><div class="phpinfo">» %s</div>',
+
+	// Logs
+	'ACP_BADGE_SAVED_LOG'		=> '<strong>Configuração de Medalhas foi guardada com sucesso</strong>',
+	'ACP_BADGE_ADD_LOG'			=> '<strong>Configuração de Medalhas Adicionada</strong>',
+	'ACP_BADGE_DELETE_LOG'		=> '<strong>Configuração de Medalhas apagada com sucesso</strong>',
+	'ACP_BADGE_IMG_DELETE_LOG'	=> '<strong>A imagem da Medalha foi removida</strong>',
+	'ACP_BADGE_IMG_UPLOAD_LOG'	=> '<strong>Foi submetida uma nova Medalha</strong>',
+));
diff --git "[ORIGINAL]/sitesplat\\badge/notification/type/award_added.php" "[FORUM_EXT]/sitesplat\\badge/notification/type/award_added.php"
--- "[ORIGINAL]/sitesplat\\badge/notification/type/award_added.php"
+++ "[FORUM_EXT]/sitesplat\\badge/notification/type/award_added.php"
@@ -94,6 +94,38 @@ class award_added extends \phpbb\notification\type\base
 		return $this->check_user_notification_options($users, $options);
 	}
 
+	/**
+	 * Get the user's avatar
+	 */
+	public function get_avatar()
+	{
+		// Define a mapping of badge types to corresponding image URLs
+		$badgeTypeToImageMap = array(
+			'BADGE_TYPE_RADAR' => 'radar.png',
+			// Add more mappings as needed
+		);
+
+		// Check if the badge type is in the mapping
+		if (isset($badgeTypeToImageMap[$this->get_data('rule_name')])) {
+			// Get the corresponding image URL
+			$imageURL = $badgeTypeToImageMap[$this->get_data('rule_name')];
+		} else {
+			// Use a default image URL if not found in the mapping
+			$imageURL = 'default.png';
+		}
+		
+		$url = generate_board_url();
+		
+		
+		// Build the URL to the badge image
+		$badgeImagePath = $url . '/ext/sitesplat/badge/badges/' . $imageURL;
+
+		// Return an HTML image tag with the badge image
+		return '<img src="' . $badgeImagePath . '" alt="Badge ' . $this->user->lang[$this->get_data('rule_name')] . '" />';
+	}
+
+
+
 	/**
 	 * {@inheritdoc}
 	 */
